<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
(6 \times r) + (r \times 4)   &= 40  \\\\
6r + 4r                       &= 40  \\\\
10r                           &= 40  \\\\
r                             &= \dfrac {40}{10} \\\\ 
r                             &= \dfrac {4 \times 10}{10} \\\\ 
r                             &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {10}}{\cancel {10}} \\\\ 
r                             &= 4  
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$4$

**Question**

Find the value of s if $\dbinom {8}{s} \wedge  \dbinom {2}{s} = 30$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the value of s if $\dbinom {8}{s} \wedge  \dbinom {2}{s} = 30$
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
(8 \times s) - (2 \times s)   &= 30  \\\\
8s - 2s                       &= 30  \\\\
6s                            &= 30  \\\\
r                             &= \dfrac {30}{6} \\\\ 
r                             &= \dfrac {5 \times 6}{6} \\\\ 
r                             &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel 6}{\cancel 6} \\\\ 
r                             &= 5  
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5$

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the value of t if $\dbinom {12}{t} \wedge  \dbinom {t}{8} = 47$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the value of t if $\dbinom {12}{t} \wedge  \dbinom {t}{8} = 47$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
(12 \times 8) - (t \times t)  &= 47  \\\\
96 - t^2                      &= 47  \\\\
t^2                           &= 96 - 47  \\\\
t^2                           &= 49 \\\\ 
t                             &= \sqrt{49} \\\\ 
t                             &= 7
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7$

</div>

                               &= 60 + 40 \\\\
                               &= \pounds 100
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the average cost per person for a party of $20$ people?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the average cost per person for a party of $20$ people?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Average cost per person} &= \dfrac {\text {Cost of 20 people}} {20} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {100} {20} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 5} {20} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{20} \times 5} {\cancel{20}} \\\\
                                &= \pounds 5
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5$

</div>
</div>

<div class="papername">

<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the missing numbers so that the answer is always $56$

![papers/the-perse-school/sample/specimen-paper-1/s01-q19-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-perse-school/sample/specimen-paper-1/s01-q19-i00.png "papers/the-perse-school/sample/specimen-paper-1/s01-q19-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the missing numbers so that the answer is always $56$
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

{/* ![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg) */}
TODOIMAGE


*TODO:: Add the image with variable written in the missing place according to the solution. Like: in 17 + ... write a, in 131 - ... write b, and so on.*

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


Let the missing number $= a, b, c, d, e$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
17 + a                                                  &= 56 \\\\
a                                                       &= 56 - 17 \\\\
a                                                       &= 39 \\\\
131 - b                                                 &= 56 \\\\
b                                                       &= 131 - 56 \\\\
b                                                       &= 75 \\\\
50 \% \ \text{of} \ c                                   &= 56 \\\\
\dfrac {50} {100} \times c                              &= 56 \\\\
\dfrac {50} {50 \times 2} \times c                      &= 56 \\\\
\dfrac {\cancel {50}} {\cancel {50} \times 2} \times c  &= 56 \\\\
\dfrac {1} {2} \times c                                 &= 56 \\\\
c                                                       &= 56 \times 2 \\\\
c                                                       &= 112 \\\\
560 \div d                                              &= 56 \\\\
d                                                       &= \dfrac {560} {56}  \\\\
d                                                       &= \dfrac {56 \times 10} {56}  \\\\
d                                                       &= \dfrac {\cancel {56} \times 10} {\cancel {56}}  \\\\
d                                                       &= 10 \\\\
\dfrac {1} {2} \times e                                 &= 56 \\\\
e                                                       &= 56 \times 2 \\\\
e                                                       &= 112
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$39, 75, 112, 10, 112$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_working_drawing_md_add_starts
*TODO:: Add the image with variable written in the missing place according to the solution. Like: in 17 + ... write a, in 131 - ... write b, and so on.*
rtq_abbr_working_drawing_md_add_ends
<emptyline>
Let the missing number $= a, b, c, d, e$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
17 + a                                                  &= 56 \\\\
a                                                       &= 56 - 17 \\\\
a                                                       &= 39 \\\\
131 - b                                                 &= 56 \\\\
b                                                       &= 131 - 56 \\\\
b                                                       &= 75 \\\\
50 \% \ \text{of} \ c                                   &= 56 \\\\
\dfrac {50} {100} \times c                              &= 56 \\\\
\dfrac {50} {50 \times 2} \times c                      &= 56 \\\\
\dfrac {\cancel {50}} {\cancel {50} \times 2} \times c  &= 56 \\\\
\dfrac {1} {2} \times c                                 &= 56 \\\\
c                                                       &= 56 \times 2 \\\\
c                                                       &= 112 \\\\
560 \div d                                              &= 56 \\\\
d                                                       &= \dfrac {560} {56}  \\\\
d                                                       &= \dfrac {56 \times 10} {56}  \\\\
d                                                       &= \dfrac {\cancel {56} \times 10} {\cancel {56}}  \\\\
d                                                       &= 10 \\\\
\dfrac {1} {2} \times e                                 &= 56 \\\\
e                                                       &= 56 \times 2 \\\\
e                                                       &= 112
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Ben wants to buy $17$ small bottles of drink for a party. A shop sells them at: $15 \ \text{p}$ for $1$ bottle; $28 \ \text{p}$ for two bottles; $60 \ \text{p}$ for  two bottles; $60 \ \text{p}$ for a pack of $5$ bottles. What is the smallest amount of money he needs to spend?  [Give your answer in $\pounds$]

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Ben wants to buy $17$ small bottles of drink for a party. A shop sells them at: $15 \ \text{p}$ for $1$ bottle; $28 \ \text{p}$ for two bottles; $60 \ \text{p}$ for  two bottles; $60 \ \text{p}$ for a pack of $5$ bottles. What is the smallest amount of money he needs to spend?  [Give your answer in $\pounds$]
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

To spend the smallest amount Ben needs to buy maximum possible pack of 5 bottles.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Bottles needed} &= 17 \\\\
                      &= 15 \ \text {bottles} + 2 \ \text {bottles} \\\\
                      &= 3 \times 5 \ \text {pack} + 1 \times 2 \ \text {pack}
\end{aligned}
$

Let's calculate the amount of money he needs to spend.

The costs are given in pence and the answer is required in pounds. Lets convert all the costs into pounds.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Smallest amount he spends}    &= 3 \times \text{Cost of 5 packs} + 1 \times \text{Cost of 2 packs} \\\\
                                    &= 3 \times 60 \ \text{p} + 1 \times 28 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                    &= 3 \times \pounds \dfrac{60}{100} + 1 \times \pounds \dfrac {28}{100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{180}{100} + \dfrac{28}{100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {208} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 2.08
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 2.08$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">To spend the smallest amount Ben needs to buy maximum possible pack of 5 bottles.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Bottles needed} &= 17 \\\\
                      &= 15 \ \text {bottles} + 2 \ \text {bottles} \\\\
                      &= 3 \times 5 \ \text {pack} + 1 \times 2 \ \text {pack}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Let's calculate the amount of money he needs to spend.
<emptyline>
The costs are given in pence and the answer is required in pounds. Lets convert all the costs into pounds.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Smallest amount he spends}    &= 3 \times \text{Cost of 5 packs} + 1 \times \text{Cost of 2 packs} \\\\
                                    &= 3 \times 60 \ \text{p} + 1 \times 28 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                    &= 3 \times \pounds \dfrac{60}{100} + 1 \times \pounds \dfrac {28}{100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{180}{100} + \dfrac{28}{100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {208} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 2.08
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On the planet Zog, all numbers are written with the digits in reverse order. For example, forty-five is written as $54$. Pluto, an inhabitant of Zog, was given the subtraction $729-26$. If no mistakes were made, what answer did Pluto write down?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On the planet Zog, all numbers are written with the digits in reverse order. For example, forty-five is written as $54$. Pluto, an inhabitant of Zog, was given the subtraction $729-26$. If no mistakes were made, what answer did Pluto write down?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 729 - 26 \\\\
&= 927 - 62 \\\\
&= 865
\end{aligned}
$

For planet Zog it will be written as $= 568$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$568$

</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the values of $A$, $B$ and $C$ in the following addition sum.

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
&         &     C      &   6   &   7       \\
&         &     7      &   4   &   A       \\ 
&    +    &     8      &   B   &   4       \\
\hline
&    2    &     0      &   0   &   6     
\end{array}
$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the values of $A$, $B$ and $C$ in the following addition sum.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
&         &     C      &   6   &   7       \\
&         &     7      &   4   &   A       \\ 
&    +    &     8      &   B   &   4       \\
\hline
&    2    &     0      &   0   &   6     
\end{array}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
7 + A + 4  &= 16 \\\\
11 + A     &= 16 \\\\
A          &= 16 - 11 \\\\
A          &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
6 + 4 + B  &= 20 \\\\
10 + B     &= 20 \\\\
B          &= 20 - 10 \\\\
B          &= 10
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
C + 7 + 8  &= 22 \\\\
15 + C     &= 22 \\\\
C          &= 22 - 15 \\\\
C          &= 7
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5, 10, 7$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If $H = 9$ and $A = 3.1$, find $R$ .

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If $H = 9$ and $A = 3.1$, find $R$ .
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
R             &= H \times (\dfrac{6570}{A^2} -1 ) \\\\
R             &= 9 \times (\dfrac{6570}{3.1^2} -1 ) \\\\
R             &= 9 \times (\dfrac{6570}{9.61} -1 ) \\\\
R             &= 9 \times (683.66 - 1) \\\\
R             &= 9 \times 682.66  \\\\
R             &= 6143.96 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6143.96$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
R             &= H \times (\dfrac{6570}{A^2} -1 ) \\\\
R             &= 9 \times (\dfrac{6570}{3.1^2} -1 ) \\\\
R             &= 9 \times (\dfrac{6570}{9.61} -1 ) \\\\
R             &= 9 \times (683.66 - 1) \\\\
R             &= 9 \times 682.66  \\\\
R             &= 6143.96 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

**Question**

If $R = 5000$ and $A = 2.3$, find $H$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If $R = 5000$ and $A = 2.3$, find $H$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
R             &= H \times (\dfrac{6570}{A^2} -1 ) \\\\
5000          &= H \times (\dfrac{6570}{2.3^2} -1 ) \\\\
5000          &= H \times (\dfrac{6570}{5.29} -1 ) \\\\
5000          &= H \times (1242 -1 ) \\\\
5000          &= H \times 1241 \\\\
H \times 1241 &= 5000 \\\\
H             &= \dfrac{5000}{1241} \\\\
H             &\approx 4
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$4$

**Question**

If $R = 8500$ and $H = 7.8$, find $A$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If $R = 8500$ and $H = 7.8$, find $A$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
R                                    &= H \times (\dfrac{6570}{A^2} -1 ) \\\\
8500                                 &= 7.8 \times (\dfrac{6570}{A^2} -1 ) \\\\
7.8 \times (\dfrac{6570}{A^2} -1 )   &= 8500 \\\\
\dfrac{6570}{A^2} -1                 &= \dfrac{8500}{7.8} \\\\
\dfrac{6570}{A^2} -1                 &= 1089.74 \\\\
\dfrac{6570}{A^2}                    &= 1090+ 1\\\\
\dfrac{6570}{A^2}                    &= 1091 \\\\
\dfrac{6570}{1091}                   &= A^2 \\\\
A^2                                  &= \dfrac{6570}{1091} \\\\
A^2                                  & \approx 6 \\\\
A                                    &= \sqrt{6} \\\\
A                                    &= 2.4
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2.4$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Solve the simultaneous equations:

$\dfrac{1}{2}x+\dfrac{1}{3}y=\dfrac{1}{4}$ 

$2x + 3y = 4$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Solve the simultaneous equations:
<emptyline>
$\dfrac{1}{2}x+\dfrac{1}{3}y=\dfrac{1}{4}$ 
<emptyline>
$2x + 3y = 4$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{1}{2}x+\dfrac{1}{3}y    &= \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
2x + 3y                        &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

We have got $2$ variables and we have $2$ equations to solve them. Let's eliminate to reduce it to just one variable. 

Multiply the first equation by $4$.

$
\begin{aligned} 
4 \times \dfrac{1}{2}x + 4 \times \dfrac{1}{3}y                             &= 4 \times \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
\dfrac{2 \times 2 }{2}x + 4 \times \dfrac{1}{3}y                            &= \cancel 4 \times \dfrac{1}{\cancel 4} \\\\
\dfrac{\cancel 2 \times 2}{\cancel 2}x + 4 \times \dfrac{1}{3}y             &= 1 \\\\
2x + \dfrac{4}{3}y                                                          &= 1
\end{aligned}
$

Subtract the above equation from the second equation to reduce it to one variable.

$
\begin{aligned} 
2x + 3y - (2x + \dfrac{4y}{3} )                                  &= 4 - 1 \\\\
2x + 3y - 2x - \dfrac{4y}{3}                                     &= 3 \\\\
3y - \dfrac{4y}{3}                                               &= 3 \\\\ 
\dfrac{3y \times 3}{1 \times 3} - \dfrac{4y}{3}                  &= 3 \\\\
\dfrac{9y - 4y}{3}                                               &= 3 \\\\
\dfrac{5y}{3}                                                     &= 3 \\\\
y                                                                &= \dfrac{3 \times 3}{5} \\\\
y                                                                &= \dfrac{9}{5} \\\\
y                                                                &= 1.8
\end{aligned}
$

Let's substitute the value of $y$ in one of the equations.

$
\begin{aligned} 
2x + 3y            &= 4 \\\\
2x + 3 \times 1.8  &= 4 \\\\
2x                 &= 4 - 5.4 \\\\
2x                 &= -1.4 \\\\
x                  &= \dfrac{1.4}{2} \\\\  
x                  &= \dfrac{1.4 \times 10 }{2 \times 10} \\\\
x                  &= -\dfrac{14 }{2 \times 10} \\\\  
x                  &= -\dfrac{2 \times 7 }{2 \times 10} \\\\ 
x                  &= -\dfrac{\cancel 2 \times 7 }{\cancel 2 \times 10} \\\\
x                  &= -\dfrac{7}{10} \\\\
x                  &= -0.7                                                   
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$-0.7, 1.8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">As per the question,
<emptyline>
$

\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A two litre bottle of lemonade costs $\pounds 1 .44$; a one and half litre  bottle costs $\pounds 1 .05$. Which seems to be better value for money - explain how you decided on your answer.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A two litre bottle of lemonade costs $\pounds 1 .44$; a one and half litre  bottle costs $\pounds 1 .05$. Which seems to be better value for money - explain how you decided on your answer.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

First case :

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of 2 litre bottle}   &= 1.44 \\\\
\text{Cost of one litre bottle}  &= \dfrac{1.44}{2} \\\\
                                 &=  \dfrac{1.44}{2} \\\\
                                 &=  \dfrac{1.44 \times 100}{2 \times 100} \\\\ 
                                 &=  \dfrac{144}{2 \times 100} \\\\
                                 &=  \dfrac{72 \times 2}{2 \times 100} \\\\ 
                                 &=  \dfrac{72 \times \cancel 2}{\cancel 2 \times 100} \\\\ 
                                 &=  \dfrac{72}{100} \\\\
                                 &=  \pounds 0.72
\end{aligned}
$

Second case :

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of one and a half litre bottle} &= 1.05 \\\\
\text{Cost of one litre bottle}             &= \dfrac{1.05 \times 2}{3} \\\\
                                            &=  \dfrac{1.05 \times 2}{3} \\\\
                                            &=  \dfrac{1.05 \times 2 \times 100}{3 \times 100} \\\\ 
                                            &=  \dfrac{105 \times 2}{3 \times 100} \\\\
                                            &=  \dfrac{35 \times 3 \times 2}{3 \times 100} \\\\ 
                                            &=  \dfrac{35 \times \cancel 3 \times 2}{\cancel 3 \times 100} \\\\ 
                                            &=  \dfrac{35 \times 2}{100} \\\\
                                            &=  \dfrac{70}{100} \\\\
                                            &=  \pounds 0.7
\end{aligned}
$

Second option seems to be the better value for money.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.05$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>

                                 &=  \dfrac{1.44 \times 100}{2 \times 100} \\\\ 
                                 &=  \dfrac{144}{2 \times 100} \\\\
                                 &=  \dfrac{72 \times 2}{2 \times 100} \\\\ 
                                 &=  \dfrac{72 \times \cancel 2}{\cancel 2 \times 100} \\\\ 
                                 &=  \dfrac{72}{100} \\\\

$

                                            &=  \dfrac{1.05 \times 2 \times 100}{3 \times 100} \\\\

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many minibuses are required to take a total of $300$ people on a trip?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many minibuses are required to take a total of $300$ people on a trip?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Minibuses required to carry 300 people}   &= \dfrac {300} {17} \\\\
                                                &= 17 \text{ remainder } 11 \\\\
                                                &\approx 18
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$18$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many empty seats will there be in total?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many empty seats will there be in total?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Empty seats}   &= \text{Total number of seats} - \text{Total number of people} \\\\
                     &= 18 \times 17 - 300 \\\\
                     &= 306 - 300 \\\\
                     &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

                     &= 306 - 300 \\\\
                     &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A minibus costs $\pounds 80$ to hire for the day. Tickets for the theme park cost $\pounds 15$ each. Write a formula for the total cost $'C'$ of hiring $'m'$ minibuses and purchasing $'t'$ tickets.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A minibus costs $\pounds 80$ to hire for the day. Tickets for the theme park cost $\pounds 15$ each. Write a formula for the total cost $'C'$ of hiring $'m'$ minibuses and purchasing $'t'$ tickets.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost per 1 minibus}                &= \pounds 80 \\\\
\text{Cost per } m \text{ minibuses}      &= 80 \times m  \\\\
                                          &= \pounds 80m \\\\
\text {Cost per 1 ticket}                 &= \pounds 15 \\\\
\text{Cost per } t \text{ tickets}        &= 15 \times t \\\\ 
                                          &= \pounds 15t \\\\
\text {Total cost }  C                    &= 80m + 15t
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$80m + 15t$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost per 1 minibus}                &= \pounds 80 \\\\
\text{Cost per } m \text{ minibuses}      &= 80 \times m  \\\\
                                          &= \pounds 80m \\\\
\text {Cost per 1 ticket}                 &= \pounds 15 \\\\
\text{Cost per } t \text{ tickets}        &= 15 \times t \\\\ 
                                          &= \pounds 15t \\\\
\text {Total cost }  C                    &= 80m + 15t
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$80m + 15t$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<div class="papername">

<div class="rag">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

To go and watch a film at the cinema, the seats cost $\pounds 3$ each for a child and $\pounds 7$ each for an adult. I was charged $\pounds 51$ for all of the seats I bought.

</div>

**Question**

Did I buy more than $7$ adult tickets?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Did I buy more than $7$ adult tickets?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of children $= a$

Let the number of adult $= b$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
3a + 7b  &= 51
\end{aligned}
$

We can easily infer the values by using trial and error. Let's find out the value of a and b which satisfies the condition above.

| $a$    |  $b$  |  $3a + 7b$  |
|:-----: |:-----:|------------:|
| $4$    | $6$   |   60        |
| $4$    | $5$   |   53        |
| $3$    | $6$   |   51        |
| $6$    | $4$   |   46        |
| $10$   | $3$   |   51        |

A quick check shows that $(3 ,6)$  and $(10, 3)$ meets all the criteria above.

In both the cases number of adults are less than $7$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

No

</div>
</div>
